[iOS][Gemini] Cap dynamic font size for revamped infobar banner button

Cap the revamped infobar banner CTA button's maximum content size
category at UIContentSizeCategoryExtraExtraLarge and resolve its font
dynamically via titleTextAttributesTransformer so UIButtonConfiguration
respects the dynamic font cap instead of using an unclamped static
font. Also preserve the button title label's multi-line and scale-to-fit
settings in configurationUpdateHandler and add vertical compression
resistance so the CTA button is not cut off at larger text sizes.

2f0832d95315558a97f2d4c9b16822d95f588a04 [ diff ] 
Test and fix for ShadowRealm last resort full gc crash

V8 full GC metrics can be attributed to a ShadowRealm context. Blink's
metrics recorder resolves that context to ShadowRealmGlobalScope and calls
UkmRecorder(), which currently hits NOTREACHED().

Return no UKM recorder and an invalid source ID for ShadowRealms. The
metrics recorder already handles contexts without a UKM recorder. Add a
regression test to the existing shadow-realm-enabled virtual suite, covering
full GC in the ordinary context, a ShadowRealm, and a nested ShadowRealm.
The web test renderer already exposes GC, so no separate suite is needed.

See also: https://github.com/electron/electron/issues/54637

Co-authored-by: Hidekazu Kondo <hidekazu.kondo@gmail.com>

tree
ef2a79c469ade35be40feb61f023db39e60ca6af
parent
26d17c7ff99f7c4777dbc6cd76884f33b3494353 [ diff ] 
[blob] Stop paging every item once memory usage is below the limit

MaybeScheduleEvictionUntilSystemHealthy() computed `total_memory_usage`
once and never updated it inside the loop. So whenever paging was
triggered, every populated item was paged to disk regardless of the
limit.

Count the bytes being paged as evicted, so paging stops once the
expected usage is at or below the limit. Paging now happens in smaller,
more frequent batches. The pressure-triggered flush (limit of 0) is
unaffected.

The StatefulMemoryPressure path is currently broken and will be fixed in
a follow-up. Its test expectations are only updated here to match the
new paging behavior.

Bug: 465068849, 570525187

[Drive Integration] Adapt Drive consent dialog for larger screens

Add multiple cosmetic improvements to the consent dialog on larger
screens:

- Use DIALOG_WHEN_LARGE to present as a floating card on tablets.
- Inset web contents on large screens to keep the SurfaceView within the
card's rounded corners.
- Tint the dialog window background to match the page baseline color.
- Cover the SurfaceView with the loading overlay on dismiss to prevent
  flicker during the window exit animation.

Before: https://screenshot-v2.corp.google.com/image/5JThrMjyrKs4t8T.png
After: https://screenshot-v2.corp.google.com/image/3F2eMZ9wzrHx4nh.png

Remove redundant browser_public_dependencies from browser, ui, and test

Now that //chrome/browser:browser has no sources or circular includes
and //chrome/browser/ui:ui has been removed, leaf targets in
//chrome/browser/BUILD.gn, //chrome/browser/ui/BUILD.gn, and
//chrome/test/BUILD.gn no longer need to depend on
//chrome/browser:browser_public_dependencies.

4266c12aa8ab6d1c7520256ef18df9cf21a126d6 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Split windows.* functions out of tabs_api

Moves the chrome.windows ExtensionFunctions and their windows-only
helpers from tabs_api.{h,cc} into new windows_api.{h,cc}. This is a
mechanical, behavior-preserving split that separates the tabs and
windows migration tracks.

MoveTabToWindow(), which is shared by windows.create and tabs.move,
moves from an anonymous namespace into tabs_internal so that both
files can use it.

Bug: 358567092

e7176147c62c93e4422a47f172617df9dadad796
parent
7916321caf8283f558183bd2d73d1daf2052b1e4 [ diff ] 
[webvtt] Keep non-snap-to-lines cues inside the video rendering area

crrev.com/c/8253934 removed the translate(-x%, -y%) from cue boxes with
a percentage line, following the spec. That transform was also what
kept such a box on screen: a line:95% cue was shifted up by 95% of its
own height. Without it the box starts at 95%, and because
AdjustPositionWithoutSnapToLines() was still empty, a second line of
text ends up below the video.

Implement the part of the snap-to-lines false branch that the
regression needs: when the cue box is not within the rendering area,
move it along the line axis to the closest position where it is. Boxes
that already fit are left untouched, so positioning only changes for
cues that were partly or fully clipped. Avoiding overlap with earlier
cues and the media controls follows in crrev.com/c/8516012.

evil/single_quote.html starts passing and its expectation line is
removed.

Bug: 568041943

c298ac300d4c8a6c19ab50b1a51d156942040bcf
parent
61acfb6d05701a1ea06e615b3db1d319b9e91590 [ diff ] 
Don't let `appearance` change `display` on non-widget elements

Only adjust `display` for elements with a native widget appearance.
Non-widget elements keep their specified `display`, while widgets such
as buttons still get the adjustment when author styles suppress their
effective appearance.

This also restores markers on <details> and <li> with `appearance:
auto`. Remove FixMarkerSuppressionForAppearanceAuto, which is superseded
by this change.

Update editing tests for the new layout: preserve the original input of
the table crash test but update its expected result; specify `display`
in the span test; update the colgroup caret expectation.

Bug: 568576344, 408002786

🌺 Revert "[WebUI NTP on AL] Enable new_tab_page_browsertest on Android"

This reverts commit 956f5117c20b1b420b7ec40be18efcc6ffcd2c30.
Culprit: 956f5117c20b - "[WebUI NTP on AL] Enable new_tab_page_browsertest on Android" (refs/heads/main@{#1716223}, https://crrev.com/c/8435150)

Reason for revert: consistently fails android-internal-desktop-x64-rel.

Mechanism: Enabling new_tab_page_browsertest on Android causes
NewTabPageTest#RealboxSearchbox and NewTabPageAppTest#RealboxNext in
android_browsertests to time out (RunLoop::Run() timed out at
ProxyRunTestOnMainThreadLoop) consistently on
android-internal-desktop-x64-rel (8/10 builds) and
android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests (4/4 builds, e.g.
https://ci.chromium.org/b/8668361486960255697).

NewTabPageTest#RealboxSearchbox, NewTabPageAppTest#RealboxNext has
failed in 8/10 recent builds of android-internal-desktop-x64-rel, every
attempt in every one of them. This is not flake: it reproduces on every
run, so it blocks the CQ for every unrelated CL that touches this suite.
The regression appears at refs/heads/main@{#1716223}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/b/8668363055189566625

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chrome.android.desktop:android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests,chromium.android.desktop:android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests|android_browsertests

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> [WebUI NTP on AL] Enable new_tab_page_browsertest on Android
>
> Enable new_tab_page_browsertest.cc and composebox_test_files on Android
> Desktop when enable_webui_ntp is true.
>
> - Move new_tab_page_browsertest.cc from !is_android to enable_webui_ntp
>   in chrome/test/data/webui/BUILD.gn, and add a direct dep on
>   composebox/variations. The test already includes
>   composebox_fieldtrial.h, and on Android nothing else provides that
>   header through public deps, so gn check fails without it.
> - Include composebox_test_files unconditionally and guard Smart Tab
>   Sharing references with <if expr="not is_android">, since its
>   searchbox.mojom methods are [EnableIfNot=is_android].
> - Add installMetricsReporterMock() in realbox_test.ts so suites with
>   reportMetrics set don't call the unmocked PageMetricsHost.
> - Enable kNtpCustomizeWebUiAndroid in NewTabPageBrowserTest on Android.
> - On Android, remove the 5s wall-clock deadline from
>   waitForAddFileCallCount. Pasted files are uploaded one at a time, and
>   each image upload makes a sync IPC to the browser UI thread
>   (URL.createObjectURL). On the Android emulator that thread can stall
>   for seconds while the soft keyboard animates, so
>   ComposeboxUploadPasteTest could miss the deadline. On Android the
>   helper now waits for the calls and asserts the exact count; the C++
>   test timeout still catches real hangs. Desktop keeps the deadline.
> - In the RealboxSearchbox tab picker test, assert right after
>   handleFuseboxAction() resolves. It opens the real context menu, and
>   yielding first lets an OS-generated window blur or resize close the
>   menu and reset shareTabsFlyoutOpen.
> - In the RealboxSearchbox entity image test, let the real image request
>   settle before mocking its load. Browser tests have no external
>   network access, so the request fails, and its error event could land
>   after the mocked load and bring back the placeholder color.
> - Disable Logo, VoiceSearchCoherence, InvariantChecks (b:565815572) and
>   ComposeboxContextMenu (b:565815899) on Android.
> - Since kNtpOneGoogleBar is disabled by default on Android, disable
>   OgbThemingRemoveScrim and OgbScrim and skip the OGB tests in Theming
>   there (b:571327206).
> - Remove the unused history_clusters include.
>
> Fixed: b:557038008

139eb78fda0e3d3e8b1169dddf570c71448a2e6d [ diff ] 
Interpret WebHID unit exponent nibble 0x8 as -8

UnitFactorExponentToInt() used a strict > comparison against 0x08, so
the 4-bit two's complement value 0x8 fell through and was reported to
pages as +8 instead of -8 for unitExponent and all six unit factor
exponents.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Ignore default-value pref writes when no cross-device entry exists
📋 Metadata
commit
703e689ef5383f48f0e6bac6348c2c9df728e900
[ log ]
author
Jenna Himawan <jhimawan@google.com>
Sat Oct 10 20:48:07 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 10 20:48:07 2026
tree
45a8bbebdd4d0f49095aa6952ab81f56db62378e
parent
8bf82f676d666de35c0fcd6ebb71e3e793e6ec4c [ diff ] 
Ignore default-value pref writes when no cross-device entry exists

Calling PrefService::Set* with a value equal to the registered default
stores an entry in the user pref store (so IsDefaultValue() returns
false) even when the effective value did not change from default (e.g.
during startup initialization).

Skip recording or propagating a cross-device pref entry when no entry
exists for the local device and the current value equals the default
pref value.

This means if a device writes a pref with a default value as part of
startup (which happens with the omnibox preference on Android),
GetBestMatchDevice methods will not count that as a true written
preference.

This bug was not relevant until after crrev.com/c/8530633 was submitted
because it used to be the case that CrossDevicePrefTracker was
instantiated after the omnibox preference was already written (which
meant that the "observed change count" for the current device would not
be incremented to 1).

02785c4323e6e8863639817c4b11ad58d30fe109

tree
071e44aa60ff9e1587aacf3fb2b950fff4cbb695
parent
fe00b152d9de52329faf2bfe6a6f698de5d3a798 [ diff ] 
[Android] Restore page focus when a tab-modal dialog closes

TabModalPresenter.saveOrRestoreTextSelection() clears focus from the
WebContents' container view whenever a tab-modal dialog is shown (not
only when text is selected), to dismiss the text selection action bar.
Nothing gave focus back when the dialog closed, so after e.g. a
permission prompt the page stayed blurred until the user tapped it.
Since crrev.com/c/8077560 this also keeps sensors suspended, so
devicemotion and deviceorientation events stop after answering a
permission prompt.

Request focus for the container view when the dialog is dismissed,
mirroring the clearFocus() call. This matches desktop, where the page
regains focus when a permission bubble closes.

ChromeTabModalPresenter only asks for this when the tab is still
interactable and selected, so focus isn't restored when a dialog is
suspended or dismissed because of a tab switch, the tab switcher, or
the tab closing.

This doesn't change the page losing focus while the dialog is showing;
only giving focus back is new.

Investigated and written with the help of Claude Code. I reviewed and
understand the change and tested it manually on a Nothing 3a running
Android 16, in touch mode, with a keyboard (Enter and Escape to dismiss
the prompt), and with a tablet-sized display (switching tabs while a
prompt is showing).

#ai-assisted

Bug: 568009418

borealis: Show disk cleanup confirmation dialog on login

Open a confirmation dialog upon login when a Borealis VM disk image is
present so the user can free disk space and uninstall borealis-dlc.
A notification is shown once cleanup completes.

tree
874d60d4a5c576b02a8d6d723f97f85413116289
parent
84c857ec6eb79d21b10566162e7b20ae835fae16 [ diff ] 
[crypto] Drop extra thread hop in MaybeMeasureTpmOperations

MaybeMeasureTpmOperations() posted its own thread pool task, but its
only caller (ChromeBrowserMainExtraPartsMetrics::PostBrowserStart())
already calls it from a delayed, CONTINUE_ON_SHUTDOWN thread pool task.
Run the measurement on the calling sequence instead, which removes a
needless thread hop.

Ash: Migrate FloatingWorkspaceService off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to
the higher-level browser application layer. This modernization also
moves code away from heavy, UI-bound Profile objects and toward the
cleaner content::BrowserContext abstraction. Where ProfileHelper wraps
user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

FloatingWorkspaceService now resolves active-user profiles through
BrowserContextHelper. This keeps desk uploads and user-switch handling
on the lower-level browser-context boundary while preserving the
existing profile identity checks.

Ash: Migrate website reporting tests off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to
the higher-level browser application layer. This modernization also
moves code away from heavy, UI-bound Profile objects and toward the
cleaner content::BrowserContext abstraction. Where ProfileHelper wraps
user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

WebsiteEventsObserverBrowserTest and
WebsiteUsageTelemetrySamplerBrowserTest now resolve their affiliated
account through BrowserContextHelper and convert the result at the
existing Profile boundary. Both fixtures log in the real affiliated
user before calling profile(), so they no longer need ProfileHelper's
testing-map wrapper or its local build and DEPS permissions.

Ash: Migrate file system provider paths off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to
the higher-level browser application layer. This modernization also
moves code away from heavy, UI-bound Profile objects and toward the
cleaner content::BrowserContext abstraction. Where ProfileHelper wraps
user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

GetMountPath() now resolves user identity through the lower-level
browser-context boundary, and URL parsing uses the matching context
classification. The affected tests register the account and annotate its
browser context. This keeps the account relationship intact without
changing session state.

AshSignalsDecoratorBrowserTest now resolves its signed-in user and
login-screen context through the lower-level browser-context layer. The
browser fixture starts with an associated stub-user context, and browser
startup loads the sign-in context before the tests run, so neither path
needs ProfileHelper's compatibility or creation behavior.

tree
6af895f2456c435404879619b1870954e369d538
parent
aa15e6ab59406a337d287cf065ef12aa60041b9b [ diff ] 
omnibox: Re-enable the popup's in-process theme colors test

OmniboxPopupUITest.PopulateLocalResourceLoaderConfig expected a
ThemeColorsSourceManager, but ThemeColorsSourceManagerFactory returns
none when kWebUIInProcessResourceLoadingV2 is disabled. The
*-no-initial-webui-rel builders disable that feature on the command
line, so the test failed there and was disabled.

The test now runs in a fixture that sets the feature explicitly, and is
parameterized over its state. With the feature off it checks that the
popup leaves chrome://theme/colors.css to the network, which the old
test did not cover.

TAG=agy
CONV=a03590ac-da78-4264-b0b6-3ac7536d2e06

3315803bded188b75fcdb8ed39087494ee775490 [ diff ] 
[Journeys] Resolve journey collections

The journey details page shows collections as sections, so resolved
journeys need them in the same form as their visits. Resolve collection
items against the journey's resolved visits. As collections are
optional, items that don't match one of the journey's visits are
dropped, as are collections left empty; neither drops the journey.

Surfacing collections in the Topics UI will follow separately.

[Journeys] Sync journey collections

Journeys can group similar pages into titled collections, which are now
stored in the history database (crrev.com/c/8529735). Fill them in from
sync so that they reach local storage, and drop them from the cached
remote specifics now that they are stored locally.

Journeys synced before this change only get collections once the server
changes them again. This is fine, since the feature is not launched yet.

NO_IFTTT=Proto fields were added in crrev.com/c/8524751

[Journeys] Share gtest printers for journey types

The resolved journey types are about to get gtest printers too. Move the
printers for the stored types out of journeys_database_unittest.cc into
a shared test-only file, so that all journey printers live in one place
and every test that prints these types uses the same printer.

d3769a25a0b237f7ba15da9727941041bca1aba4
parent
967c1cbec0c212dee6dd3cab7edf55e82731ee13 [ diff ] 
printing: Bind target frame ID at initiatePrint on Android

Android's 3-stage print lifecycle previously used -1/-1 sentinel frame
coordinates when initiated from the browser menu, re-resolving the
target frame on each JNI hop. If the initiating frame navigated or
entered BackForwardCache before dialog dismissal, finishPrint() targeted
the new primary main frame instead of the frame that entered printing
mode, leaving the cached frame stuck in printing state.

Bind the print session to the target frame's GlobalRenderFrameHostId at
session initiation and propagate that identity across subsequent print()
and finishPrint() calls. Decouple FinishPrint() lookup from active-frame
selection so teardown always reaches the originating frame as long as it
remains live.

TAG=agy
CONV=481a86f4-576b-401a-b6ab-ac7b7a0fd3e1

Bug: 562231433

0ad46f49ac03fcb28c79115ec48c70fb86cad82e [ diff ] 
[CRD host] Validate calling extension origin in remote_webauthn

remote_webauthn only verified that it was launched by Google Chrome.
A user-level native messaging host manifest could point to the
genuine remote_webauthn binary and list an arbitrary extension in
allowed_origins, letting that extension drive the WebAuthn proxy.

Chrome always passes the calling extension origin as the first
positional argument to native messaging hosts, so remote_webauthn now
rejects callers whose origin is not one of the CRD WebAuthn
extensions. The extension ID list is moved from
RemoteWebAuthnExtensionNotifier to remote_webauthn_constants so that
it can be shared by both.

Note that, consistent with the generated NMH manifest, release builds
only accept the prod extensions, so a local release build of
remote_webauthn will reject the dev extensions even though
register_local_nm_hosts.sh registers them.

Bug: 533070896
